context/ega_fr/EGA_chap0_ega0-16.tex:210:Recall that there exists a \emph{polynomial} $\mathrm{Q}(n)$ with rational coefficients such that $\operatorname{long}(\gr_n(\mathrm{M}))=\mathrm{Q}(n)$ for $n$ sufficiently large $(\mathbf{III},~2.5.4)$; from this one deduces immediately that there exists a polynomial $\mathrm{P}_\mathfrak{q}(\mathrm{M},n)$ in $n$, with rational coefficients, such that $\operatorname{long}(\mathrm{M}/\mathfrak{q}^n\mathrm{M})=\mathrm{P}_\mathfrak{q}(\mathrm{M},n)$ \emph{for $n$ sufficiently large}; it is clear that this polynomial is unique and that its leading coefficient is $>0$ if $\mathrm{M}\neq 0$; we call it the \emph{Hilbert--Samuel polynomial} of $\mathrm{M}$ for the $\mathfrak{q}$-preadic filtration.
